# === FILE: apps\examination\models.py ===
from decimal import Decimal
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from django.db import models
from django.utils import timezone
from apps.academics.models import AcademicSection, Subject
from apps.common.models import BaseModel
from apps.users_auth.models import StudentProfile, User

class Exam(BaseModel):
    """
    কলেজের মূল পরীক্ষার কনফিগারেশন মডেল।
    """
    class ExamType(models.TextChoices):
        SEMESTER = 'SEMESTER', 'সেমিস্টার পরীক্ষা (Semester Exam)'
        MODEL_TEST = 'MODEL_TEST', 'মডেল টেস্ট (Model Test - No Tutorial)'
        ANNUAL = 'ANNUAL', 'বার্ষিক পরীক্ষা (Annual Exam)'
        PRE_TEST = 'PRE_TEST', 'প্রাক-নির্বাচনী পরীক্ষা (Pre-Test)'
        TEST = 'TEST', 'নির্বাচনী পরীক্ষা (Test Exam)'

    name = models.CharField(max_length=150, help_text="যেমন: 2nd Semester - 2025")
    exam_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=ExamType.choices, default=ExamType.SEMESTER, db_index=True)
    session = models.CharField(max_length=20, db_index=True, help_text="যেমন: 2025-2026")
    class_level = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=AcademicSection.ClassLevel.choices, db_index=True)
    
    start_date = models.DateField(help_text="পরীক্ষা বা সেমিস্টার শুরুর তারিখ (হাজিরা গণনার জন্য)")
    end_date = models.DateField(help_text="পরীক্ষা শেষের তারিখ")

    # ফ্লেক্সিবল কনভার্সন অপশন (মডেল টেস্টে টিউটোরিয়াল ছাড়া ১০০%, সেমিস্টারে ৮০% + ২০%)
    has_tutorial_conversion = models.BooleanField(
        default=True,
        help_text="টিক দেওয়া থাকলে ৮০% এক্সাম + ২০% টিউটোরিয়াল হবে। আনচেক থাকলে সরাসরি ১০০% এক্সাম নম্বর কাউন্ট হবে।"
    )
    exam_weight_percentage = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=80.00)
    tutorial_weight_percentage = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=20.00)

    is_published = models.BooleanField(default=False, db_index=True, help_text="টিক দিলে ফলাফল শিক্ষার্থীদের জন্য দৃশ্যমান হবে")
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Exam'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Exams'
        ordering = ['-start_date']

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if self.exam_type == self.ExamType.MODEL_TEST:
            self.has_tutorial_conversion = False
            self.exam_weight_percentage = Decimal('100.00')
            self.tutorial_weight_percentage = Decimal('0.00')
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.name} [{self.get_class_level_display()}] ({self.session})"


class ExamSubjectConfig(BaseModel):
    """
    পরীক্ষায় বিষয়ের পূর্ণমান ও নম্বর বিভাজন (CQ, MCQ, Practical, Tutorial)।
    """
    exam = models.ForeignKey(Exam, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='subject_configs')
    subject = models.ForeignKey(Subject, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='exam_configs')
    
    full_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=100.00)
    cq_full_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=70.00)
    mcq_full_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=30.00)
    practical_full_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    tutorial_full_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=20.00)
    pass_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=33.00)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Exam Subject Configuration'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Exam Subject Configurations'
        unique_together = ('exam', 'subject')

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.exam.name} - {self.subject.name} (Full: {self.full_marks})"


class SubjectMarksEntry(BaseModel):
    """
    বিষয়ভিত্তিক শিক্ষার্থীদের প্রাপ্ত নম্বর এন্ট্রি টেবিল।
    শিক্ষক একই টেবিলে CQ, MCQ, Practical এবং Tutorial নম্বর এন্ট্রি করবেন।
    """
    exam = models.ForeignKey(Exam, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='marks_entries')
    subject = models.ForeignKey(Subject, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='marks_entries')
    student = models.ForeignKey(StudentProfile, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='subject_marks')
    section = models.ForeignKey(AcademicSection, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='subject_marks')
    
    # শিক্ষকের ইনপুট ফিল্ড
    cq_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    mcq_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    practical_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    tutorial_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    is_absent = models.BooleanField(default=False)

    # সিস্টেম কর্তৃক স্বয়ংক্রিয়ভাবে হিসাবকৃত ফলাফল
    obtained_semester_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00) # CQ + MCQ + Practical
    converted_semester_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00) # 80% (or 100%)
    final_total_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00) # Converted + Tutorial
    
    grade_letter = models.CharField(max_length=5, default='F', db_index=True) # A+, A, A-, B, C, D, F
    grade_point = models.DecimalField(max_digits=4, decimal_places=2, default=0.00) # 5.0, 4.0 ... 0.0
    is_passed = models.BooleanField(default=False, db_index=True)

    entered_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Subject Marks Entry'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Subject Marks Entries'
        unique_together = ('exam', 'subject', 'student')
        ordering = ['student__roll_number']

    def calculate_results(self):
        if self.is_absent:
            self.cq_marks = Decimal('0.00')
            self.mcq_marks = Decimal('0.00')
            self.practical_marks = Decimal('0.00')
            self.tutorial_marks = Decimal('0.00')
            self.obtained_semester_marks = Decimal('0.00')
            self.converted_semester_marks = Decimal('0.00')
            self.final_total_marks = Decimal('0.00')
            self.grade_letter = 'F'
            self.grade_point = Decimal('0.00')
            self.is_passed = False
            return

        # ১. সেমিস্টারের মূল লিখিত ও ব্যবহারিক নম্বর
        self.obtained_semester_marks = self.cq_marks + self.mcq_marks + self.practical_marks

        # ২. কনভার্সন শতকরা হিসাব
        if self.exam.has_tutorial_conversion:
            weight_factor = self.exam.exam_weight_percentage / Decimal('100.00')
            self.converted_semester_marks = round(self.obtained_semester_marks * weight_factor, 1)
            self.final_total_marks = round(self.converted_semester_marks + self.tutorial_marks, 1)
        else:
            self.converted_semester_marks = self.obtained_semester_marks
            self.final_total_marks = self.obtained_semester_marks

        # ৩. বাংলাদেশ শিক্ষা বোর্ড গ্রেডিং স্কেল
        total = self.final_total_marks
        if total >= Decimal('80.00'):
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'A+', Decimal('5.00')
        elif total >= Decimal('70.00'):
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'A', Decimal('4.00')
        elif total >= Decimal('60.00'):
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'A-', Decimal('3.50')
        elif total >= Decimal('50.00'):
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'B', Decimal('3.00')
        elif total >= Decimal('40.00'):
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'C', Decimal('2.00')
        elif total >= Decimal('33.00'):
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'D', Decimal('1.00')
        else:
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'F', Decimal('0.00')

        self.is_passed = (self.grade_letter != 'F')

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        self.calculate_results()
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.student.roll_number} - {self.subject.name}: {self.final_total_marks} ({self.grade_letter})"


class CoCurricularEntry(BaseModel):
    """
    সহ-পাঠ্যক্রমিক কার্যক্রম (Co-curricular Activity, পূর্ণমান ১২৪):
    Attn (হাজিরা) স্বয়ংক্রিয়ভাবে Sprint 3-এর হাজিরা থেকে আসবে, এবং Ethics ইনচার্জ এন্ট্রি দেবেন।
    """
    exam = models.ForeignKey(Exam, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='cocurricular_entries')
    student = models.ForeignKey(StudentProfile, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='cocurricular_entries')
    section = models.ForeignKey(AcademicSection, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='cocurricular_entries')

    attendance_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00, help_text="ডিজিটাল হাজিরা থেকে অটো হিসাবকৃত")
    ethics_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00, help_text="শ্রেণি ইনচার্জ কর্তৃক প্রদত্ত")
    total_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    
    grade_letter = models.CharField(max_length=5, default='F')
    grade_point = models.DecimalField(max_digits=4, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Co-Curricular Entry'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Co-Curricular Entries'
        unique_together = ('exam', 'student')

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        self.total_marks = round(self.attendance_marks + self.ethics_marks, 2)
        # ১২৪ নম্বরের ওপর ভিত্তি করে শতকরা হিসাব: (প্রাপ্ত নম্বর / ১২৪) * ১০০
        scaled_score = (self.total_marks / Decimal('124.00')) * Decimal('100.00') if self.total_marks > 0 else Decimal('0.00')

        if scaled_score >= Decimal('80.00'):
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'A+', Decimal('5.00')
        elif scaled_score >= Decimal('70.00'):
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'A', Decimal('4.00')
        elif scaled_score >= Decimal('60.00'):
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'A-', Decimal('3.50')
        elif scaled_score >= Decimal('50.00'):
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'B', Decimal('3.00')
        elif scaled_score >= Decimal('40.00'):
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'C', Decimal('2.00')
        elif scaled_score >= Decimal('33.00'):
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'D', Decimal('1.00')
        else:
            self.grade_letter, self.grade_point = 'F', Decimal('0.00')

        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.student.roll_number} - Co-curricular: {self.total_marks} ({self.grade_letter})"


class StudentExamResult(BaseModel):
    """
    শিক্ষার্থীর সামগ্রিক পরীক্ষার চূড়ান্ত ফলাফল, জিপিএ, মোট নম্বর এবং মেধা স্থান (Merit Position)।
    """
    exam = models.ForeignKey(Exam, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='processed_results')
    student = models.ForeignKey(StudentProfile, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='exam_results')
    section = models.ForeignKey(AcademicSection, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='exam_results')

    total_full_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=7, decimal_places=2, default=824.00, help_text="৭০০ + ১২৪ = ৮২৪")
    total_obtained_marks = models.DecimalField(max_digits=7, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    
    gpa = models.DecimalField(max_digits=4, decimal_places=2, default=0.00, db_index=True)
    grade_letter = models.CharField(max_length=5, default='F', db_index=True)
    is_passed = models.BooleanField(default=False, db_index=True)

    failed_subjects_count = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)
    failed_subject_names = models.TextField(blank=True, help_text="অকৃতকার্য বিষয়ের কমা-সেপারেটেড নাম")

    # মেধা তালিকা স্থান
    section_position = models.PositiveIntegerField(null=True, blank=True, db_index=True)
    class_position = models.PositiveIntegerField(null=True, blank=True, db_index=True)

    # পরীক্ষার সময়সীমার মধ্যকার উপস্থিতি সামারি
    working_days = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)
    present_days = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)
    absent_days = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)

    processed_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Student Exam Result'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Student Exam Results'
        unique_together = ('exam', 'student')
        ordering = ['-is_passed', '-gpa', '-total_obtained_marks', 'student__roll_number']
        indexes = [
            # রেজাল্টের দিন এক ক্লিকে মেধা তালিকা লোড হওয়া
            models.Index(fields=['exam', 'is_passed', '-gpa']),
            models.Index(fields=['section', 'is_passed']),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.student.roll_number} - {self.student.user.get_full_name()} | GPA: {self.gpa} ({self.grade_letter}) - Pos: Sec {self.section_position}, Class {self.class_position}"


class ExamRoom(BaseModel):
    """
    পরীক্ষার হলের রুম তালিকা ও আসন ধারণক্ষমতা।
    """
    room_number = models.CharField(max_length=50, unique=True, help_text="যেমন: Room-201, Room-202")
    building = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, default='Main Building')
    capacity = models.PositiveIntegerField(help_text="এই রুমে মোট কতজন শিক্ষার্থী পরীক্ষা দিতে পারবে")
    benches_count = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=20)
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Exam Room'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Exam Rooms'
        ordering = ['room_number']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.room_number} (Capacity: {self.capacity})"


class ExamSeatAssignment(BaseModel):
    """
    স্বয়ংক্রিয় সিট প্ল্যান অনুযায়ী শিক্ষার্থীর নির্ধারিত পরীক্ষার হল ও সিট নম্বর।
    """
    exam = models.ForeignKey(Exam, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='seat_plans')
    student = models.ForeignKey(StudentProfile, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='exam_seats')
    room = models.ForeignKey(ExamRoom, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='seated_students')
    seat_number = models.PositiveIntegerField(help_text="রুমের ভেতর নির্দিষ্ট আসন নম্বর")

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Exam Seat Assignment'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Exam Seat Assignments'
        unique_together = ('exam', 'student')
        ordering = ['room', 'seat_number']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.student.roll_number} - {self.room.room_number} (Seat: {self.seat_number})"


class InvigilationDuty(BaseModel):
    """
    পরীক্ষায় শিক্ষকদের হল গার্ড বা ইনভিজিলেশন রোস্টার।
    """
    exam = models.ForeignKey(Exam, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='invigilation_duties')
    room = models.ForeignKey(ExamRoom, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='duty_teachers')
    teacher = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='exam_duties',
        limit_choices_to={'role': User.Role.TEACHER}
    )
    duty_date = models.DateField(db_index=True)
    start_time = models.TimeField()
    end_time = models.TimeField()
    remarks = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Invigilation Duty'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Invigilation Duties'
        ordering = ['duty_date', 'start_time']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.teacher.get_full_name()} - {self.room.room_number} [{self.duty_date}]"


class QuestionPaperSubmission(BaseModel):
    """
    শিক্ষক কর্তৃক পাসওয়ার্ড-প্রটেক্টেড প্রশ্নপত্র আপলোড ও প্রিন্সিপালের তদারকি।
    """
    class PaperStatus(models.TextChoices):
        SUBMITTED = 'SUBMITTED', 'জমা দেওয়া হয়েছে (Submitted)'
        ACCEPTED = 'ACCEPTED', 'গৃহীত (Accepted)'
        REVISION_REQUIRED = 'REVISION', 'সংশোধন প্রয়োজন (Revision Required)'

    exam = models.ForeignKey(Exam, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='question_papers')
    subject = models.ForeignKey(Subject, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='question_papers')
    teacher = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='submitted_questions')
    
    question_file = models.FileField(upload_to='question_papers/%Y/', help_text="পাসওয়ার্ড প্রটেক্টেড PDF বা Doc ফাইল")
    file_password_hint = models.CharField(max_length=150, blank=True, help_text="প্রশ্নপত্রের পাসওয়ার্ড (শুধুমাত্র প্রিন্সিপাল দেখতে পাবেন)")
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=PaperStatus.choices, default=PaperStatus.SUBMITTED)
    principal_remarks = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Question Paper Submission'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Question Paper Submissions'
        unique_together = ('exam', 'subject')

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.subject.name} - {self.exam.name} ({self.status})"

    ExamSubjectConfig,

)

class SingleStudentMarkItemSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    student_id = serializers.UUIDField()
    cq_marks = serializers.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    mcq_marks = serializers.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    practical_marks = serializers.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    tutorial_marks = serializers.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    is_absent = serializers.BooleanField(default=False)


class BulkSubjectMarksEntrySerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    """
    বিষয়ভিত্তিক শিক্ষক কর্তৃক ক্লাসের সব স্টুডেন্টের CQ, MCQ, Practical, Tutorial এক ক্লিকে এন্ট্রি করার সিরিয়ালাইজার।
    """
    exam_id = serializers.UUIDField()
    subject_id = serializers.UUIDField()
    section_id = serializers.UUIDField()
    marks_list = SingleStudentMarkItemSerializer(many=True)

    def validate(self, attrs):
        if not Exam.objects.filter(id=attrs['exam_id']).exists():
            raise serializers.ValidationError({"exam_id": "প্রদত্ত পরীক্ষাটি খুঁজে পাওয়া যায়নি।"})
        if not Subject.objects.filter(id=attrs['subject_id']).exists():
            raise serializers.ValidationError({"subject_id": "প্রদত্ত বিষয়টি খুঁজে পাওয়া যায়নি।"})
        if not AcademicSection.objects.filter(id=attrs['section_id']).exists():
            raise serializers.ValidationError({"section_id": "প্রদত্ত শ্রেণি/শাখাটি খুঁজে পাওয়া যায়নি।"})
        return attrs

    @transaction.atomic
    def save_marks(self, teacher_user):
        exam = Exam.objects.get(id=self.validated_data['exam_id'])
        subject = Subject.objects.get(id=self.validated_data['subject_id'])
        section = AcademicSection.objects.get(id=self.validated_data['section_id'])
        marks_list = self.validated_data['marks_list']

        saved_entries = []
        for item in marks_list:
            entry, _ = SubjectMarksEntry.objects.update_or_create(
                exam=exam,
                subject=subject,
                student_id=item['student_id'],
                defaults={
                    'section': section,
                    'cq_marks': item.get('cq_marks', Decimal('0.00')),
                    'mcq_marks': item.get('mcq_marks', Decimal('0.00')),
                    'practical_marks': item.get('practical_marks', Decimal('0.00')),
                    'tutorial_marks': item.get('tutorial_marks', Decimal('0.00')),
                    'is_absent': item.get('is_absent', False),
                    'entered_by': teacher_user,
                }
            )
            saved_entries.append(entry)

        return saved_entries


class SingleEthicsItemSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    student_id = serializers.UUIDField()
    ethics_marks = serializers.DecimalField(max_digits=6, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)


class BulkCoCurricularEntrySerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    """
    শ্রেণি ইনচার্জ কর্তৃক Co-curricular নম্বর তৈরি:
    হাজিরা স্বয়ংক্রিয়ভাবে ডিজিটাল হাজিরা থেকে ক্যালকুলেট হবে এবং ইনচার্জের পাঠানো Ethics নম্বর যুক্ত হবে।
    """
    exam_id = serializers.UUIDField()
    section_id = serializers.UUIDField()
    ethics_list = SingleEthicsItemSerializer(many=True)

    @transaction.atomic
    def save_co_curricular(self):
        exam = Exam.objects.get(id=self.validated_data['exam_id'])
        section = AcademicSection.objects.get(id=self.validated_data['section_id'])
        ethics_list = self.validated_data['ethics_list']

        # পরীক্ষার সময়সীমার মধ্যকার মোট কার্যদিবস ও উপস্থিতির তথ্য সংগ্রহ
        total_days = StudentAttendance.objects.filter(
            section=section,
            date__range=[exam.start_date, exam.end_date]
        ).values('date').distinct().count()

        saved_entries = []
        for item in ethics_list:
            student = StudentProfile.objects.get(id=item['student_id'])
            
            # ডিজিটাল হাজিরা থেকে স্বয়ংক্রিয় উপস্থিতি গণনা
            if total_days > 0:
                present_days = StudentAttendance.objects.filter(
                    student=student,
                    date__range=[exam.start_date, exam.end_date],
                    status__in=[StudentAttendance.AttendanceStatus.PRESENT, StudentAttendance.AttendanceStatus.LATE]
                ).count()
                # সর্বোচ্চ ৬০ নম্বরের মধ্যে হাজিরার স্কেলিং
                calculated_attn = round((Decimal(present_days) / Decimal(total_days)) * Decimal('60.00'), 1)
            else:
                calculated_attn = Decimal('0.00')

            cocurricular, _ = CoCurricularEntry.objects.update_or_create(
                exam=exam,
                student=student,
                defaults={
                    'section': section,
                    'attendance_marks': calculated_attn,
                    'ethics_marks': item.get('ethics_marks', Decimal('0.00')),
                }
            )
            saved_entries.append(cocurricular)

        return saved_entries

from django.urls import include, path
from rest_framework.routers import DefaultRouter
from apps.examination.views import (
    ExamRoomViewSet,
    ExamSubjectConfigViewSet,
    ExamViewSet,
    ExaminationReportsViewSet,
    InvigilationDutyViewSet,
    MarksEntryViewSet,
    QuestionPaperSubmissionViewSet,
    StudentExamResultViewSet,
)

app_name = 'examination'

router = DefaultRouter()
router.register(r'exams', ExamViewSet, basename='exam')
router.register(r'configs', ExamSubjectConfigViewSet, basename='exam-config')
router.register(r'marks', MarksEntryViewSet, basename='marks')
router.register(r'results', StudentExamResultViewSet, basename='result')
router.register(r'rooms', ExamRoomViewSet, basename='exam-room')
router.register(r'invigilations', InvigilationDutyViewSet, basename='invigilation')
router.register(r'questions', QuestionPaperSubmissionViewSet, basename='question-paper')
router.register(r'reports', ExaminationReportsViewSet, basename='report')

urlpatterns = [
    path('', include(router.urls)),
]

from decimal import Decimal
from django.db import transaction
from django.db.models import Count, Max, Q, Sum
from rest_framework import status, viewsets
from rest_framework.decorators import action
from rest_framework.permissions import IsAuthenticated
from rest_framework.response import Response

from apps.academics.models import AcademicSection, StudentAttendance, Subject
from apps.common.permissions import IsPrincipal, IsTeacherOrPrincipal
from apps.examination.models import (
    CoCurricularEntry,
    Exam,
    ExamRoom,
    ExamSeatAssignment,
    ExamSubjectConfig,
    InvigilationDuty,
    QuestionPaperSubmission,
    StudentExamResult,
    SubjectMarksEntry,
)
from apps.examination.serializers import (
    BulkCoCurricularEntrySerializer,
    BulkSubjectMarksEntrySerializer,
    ExamRoomSerializer,
    ExamSeatAssignmentSerializer,
    ExamSerializer,
    ExamSubjectConfigSerializer,
    InvigilationDutySerializer,
    QuestionPaperSubmissionSerializer,
    ResultProcessRequestSerializer,
    StudentExamResultSerializer,
)
from apps.finance.models import StudentInvoice
from apps.users_auth.models import StudentProfile, User


class ExamViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    """
    পরীক্ষা তৈরি ও কনফিগারেশন ভিউসেট (প্রিন্সিপাল পরিচালিত)।
    """
    queryset = Exam.objects.all()
    serializer_class = ExamSerializer

    def get_permissions(self):
        if self.action in ['list', 'retrieve', 'my_admit_card']:
            return [IsAuthenticated()]
        return [IsPrincipal()]

    def perform_create(self, serializer):
        serializer.save(created_by=self.request.user)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['get'], url_path='my-admit-card')
    def my_admit_card(self, request, pk=None):
        """
        GET /api/v1/examination/exams/<id>/my-admit-card/
        স্মার্ট অ্যাডমিট কার্ড ইঞ্জিন (Due-Lock লজিক):
        কোনো বকেয়া থাকলে সরাসরি 403 Forbidden সহ লাল সতর্কবার্তা আসবে।
        """
        if request.user.role != User.Role.STUDENT or not hasattr(request.user, 'student_profile'):
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "শুধুমাত্র শিক্ষার্থীরা অ্যাডমিট কার্ড পেতে পারে।"}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        exam = self.get_object()
        student = request.user.student_profile

        # বকেয়া চেক (Due-Lock Logic)
        total_due = StudentInvoice.objects.filter(
            student=student,
            due_amount__gt=Decimal('0.00')
        ).aggregate(total=Sum('due_amount'))['total'] or Decimal('0.00')

        if total_due > Decimal('0.00'):
            return Response({
                "success": False,
                "is_locked": True,
                "message": "অনুগ্রহপূর্বক তোমার শ্রেণি ইনচার্জের কাছে বকেয়া পরিশোধ করে অ্যাডমিট কার্ড আনলক করো।",
                "total_due_amount": float(total_due),
                "warning": "বকেয়া ফি পরিশোধ ব্যতীত পরীক্ষায় অংশগ্রহণ নিষিদ্ধ।"
            }, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        seat_assignment = ExamSeatAssignment.objects.filter(exam=exam, student=student).first()
        subject_configs = exam.subject_configs.select_related('subject')
        routine_slots = [
            {
                "subject_name": sc.subject.name,
                "subject_code": sc.subject.code,
                "full_marks": float(sc.full_marks),
            }
            for sc in subject_configs
        ]

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "is_locked": False,
            "institution": {
                "name": "STARLIGHT COLLEGE",
                "address": "Sunargown R/A, Dokshin surma, Sylhet",
                "eiin": "135342",
            },
            "admit_card": {
                "exam_name": exam.name,
                "session": exam.session,
                "student_name": student.user.get_full_name(),
                "student_id": student.student_unique_id,
                "roll_number": student.roll_number,
                "class_level": student.section.get_class_level_display(),
                "group": student.section.get_group_display(),
                "section": student.section.section_name,
                "seat_info": {
                    "room_number": seat_assignment.room.room_number if seat_assignment else "বরাদ্দ হয়নি",
                    "seat_number": seat_assignment.seat_number if seat_assignment else "অপেক্ষমাণ",
                },
                "subjects": routine_slots,
                "instructions": [
                    "পরীক্ষা শুরুর ১৫ মিনিট পূর্বে পরীক্ষার হলে উপস্থিত হতে হবে।",
                    "অ্যাডমিট কার্ড ছাড়া কোনো শিক্ষার্থীকে পরীক্ষার হলে প্রবেশ করতে দেওয়া হবে না।",
                    "মোবাইল ফোন বা কোনো ডিজিটাল ডিভাইস সম্পূর্ণ নিষিদ্ধ।"
                ]
            }
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['post'], permission_classes=[IsPrincipal], url_path='generate-seat-plan')
    @transaction.atomic
    def generate_seat_plan(self, request, pk=None):
        """
        POST /api/v1/examination/exams/<id>/generate-seat-plan/
        স্বয়ংক্রিয় সিট প্ল্যান জেনারেটর (রুমের ধারণক্ষমতা অনুযায়ী ক্রমানুসারে আসন বণ্টন)।
        """
        exam = self.get_object()
        room_ids = request.data.get('room_ids', [])

        if not room_ids:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "কমপক্ষে একটি রুম নির্বাচন করা আবশ্যক।"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        rooms = ExamRoom.objects.filter(id__in=room_ids, is_active=True).order_by('room_number')
        students = StudentProfile.objects.filter(section__class_level=exam.class_level, user__is_active=True).order_by('roll_number')

        total_capacity = sum(r.capacity for r in rooms)
        if len(students) > total_capacity:
            return Response({
                "success": False,
                "message": f"নির্বাচিত রুমগুলোতে মোট আসন {total_capacity}টি, কিন্তু পরীক্ষার্থী রয়েছে {len(students)} জন। আরও রুম যুক্ত করুন।"
            }, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        ExamSeatAssignment.objects.filter(exam=exam).delete()

        student_idx = 0
        assigned_records = []

        for room in rooms:
            for seat_no in range(1, room.capacity + 1):
                if student_idx >= len(students):
                    break
                
                assignment = ExamSeatAssignment(
                    exam=exam,
                    student=students[student_idx],
                    room=room,
                    seat_number=seat_no
                )
                assigned_records.append(assignment)
                student_idx += 1

        ExamSeatAssignment.objects.bulk_create(assigned_records)

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"{exam.name} এর জন্য মোট {len(assigned_records)} জন শিক্ষার্থীর আসন বিন্যাস সফলভাবে তৈরি করা হয়েছে।",
            "total_seated": len(assigned_records),
            "rooms_used": rooms.count()
        }, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)


class ExamRoomViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    queryset = ExamRoom.objects.all()
    serializer_class = ExamRoomSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsPrincipal]


class InvigilationDutyViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    """
    শিক্ষকদের হল গার্ড রোস্টার ভিউসেট।
    """
    queryset = InvigilationDuty.objects.select_related('exam', 'room', 'teacher').all()
    serializer_class = InvigilationDutySerializer
    permission_classes = [IsTeacherOrPrincipal]

    def get_queryset(self):
        user = self.request.user
        queryset = super().get_queryset()
        if user.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            return queryset.filter(teacher=user)
        return queryset


class QuestionPaperSubmissionViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    """
    শিক্ষক কর্তৃক পাসওয়ার্ড-প্রটেক্টেড প্রশ্নপত্র আপলোড।
    """
    queryset = QuestionPaperSubmission.objects.select_related('exam', 'subject', 'teacher').all()
    serializer_class = QuestionPaperSubmissionSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsTeacherOrPrincipal]

    def perform_create(self, serializer):
        serializer.save(teacher=self.request.user)


class ExamSubjectConfigViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    """
    পরীক্ষার বিষয়ভিত্তিক পূর্ণমান ও পাশ নম্বর নির্ধারণ।
    """
    queryset = ExamSubjectConfig.objects.select_related('exam', 'subject').all()
    serializer_class = ExamSubjectConfigSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsPrincipal]


class MarksEntryViewSet(viewsets.GenericViewSet):
    permission_classes = [IsTeacherOrPrincipal]

    @action(detail=False, methods=['get'], url_path='mark-sheet')
    def get_mark_sheet(self, request):
        exam_id = request.query_params.get('exam')
        subject_id = request.query_params.get('subject')
        section_id = request.query_params.get('section')

        if not exam_id or not subject_id or not section_id:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "exam, subject এবং section প্যারামিটার প্রয়োজন।"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        students = StudentProfile.objects.filter(section_id=section_id, user__is_active=True).select_related('user').order_by('roll_number')
        existing_marks = {
            m.student_id: m
            for m in SubjectMarksEntry.objects.filter(exam_id=exam_id, subject_id=subject_id, section_id=section_id)
        }

        sheet = []
        for s in students:
            m = existing_marks.get(s.id)
            sheet.append({
                "student_id": str(s.id),
                "student_unique_id": s.student_unique_id,
                "roll_number": s.roll_number,
                "student_name": s.user.get_full_name() or s.user.username,
                "cq_marks": float(m.cq_marks) if m else 0.0,
                "mcq_marks": float(m.mcq_marks) if m else 0.0,
                "practical_marks": float(m.practical_marks) if m else 0.0,
                "tutorial_marks": float(m.tutorial_marks) if m else 0.0,
                "final_total_marks": float(m.final_total_marks) if m else 0.0,
                "grade_letter": m.grade_letter if m else 'N/A',
                "is_absent": m.is_absent if m else False,
            })

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "total_students": len(sheet),
            "students": sheet
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=False, methods=['post'], url_path='bulk-save')
    def bulk_save_marks(self, request):
        serializer = BulkSubjectMarksEntrySerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        saved = serializer.save_marks(teacher_user=request.user)

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"মোট {len(saved)} জন শিক্ষার্থীর নম্বর ও গ্রেড সফলভাবে হিসাব করে সংরক্ষণ করা হয়েছে।",
            "saved_count": len(saved)
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=False, methods=['post'], url_path='save-cocurricular')
    def save_cocurricular(self, request):
        serializer = BulkCoCurricularEntrySerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        saved = serializer.save_co_curricular()

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"মোট {len(saved)} জন শিক্ষার্থীর সহ-পাঠ্যক্রমিক কার্যক্রম ফলাফল সফলভাবে তৈরি করা হয়েছে।",
            "saved_count": len(saved)
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class StudentExamResultViewSet(viewsets.ReadOnlyModelViewSet):
    queryset = StudentExamResult.objects.select_related('exam', 'student', 'student__user', 'section').all()
    serializer_class = StudentExamResultSerializer

    def get_permissions(self):
        if self.action == 'process_result':
            return [IsPrincipal()]
        return [IsAuthenticated()]

    def get_queryset(self):
        user = self.request.user
        queryset = super().get_queryset()

        if user.role == User.Role.STUDENT and hasattr(user, 'student_profile'):
            return queryset.filter(student=user.student_profile, exam__is_published=True)

        if user.role == User.Role.TEACHER:
            return queryset.filter(section__in_charge_teacher=user)

        exam_id = self.request.query_params.get('exam')
        if exam_id:
            queryset = queryset.filter(exam_id=exam_id)

        section_id = self.request.query_params.get('section')
        if section_id:
            queryset = queryset.filter(section_id=section_id)

        return queryset

    @action(detail=False, methods=['post'], url_path='process')
    @transaction.atomic
    def process_result(self, request):
        serializer = ResultProcessRequestSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        exam_id = serializer.validated_data['exam_id']
        section_id = serializer.validated_data.get('section_id')

        exam = Exam.objects.get(id=exam_id)
        students_qs = StudentProfile.objects.filter(section__class_level=exam.class_level, user__is_active=True)

        if section_id:
            students_qs = students_qs.filter(section_id=section_id)

        working_days = StudentAttendance.objects.filter(
            section__class_level=exam.class_level,
            date__range=[exam.start_date, exam.end_date]
        ).values('date').distinct().count()

        processed_results = []
        for student in students_qs:
            marks_entries = SubjectMarksEntry.objects.filter(exam=exam, student=student)
            cocurricular = CoCurricularEntry.objects.filter(exam=exam, student=student).first()

            total_obtained = sum(m.final_total_marks for m in marks_entries)
            failed_subjects = [m.subject.name for m in marks_entries if not m.is_passed]

            if cocurricular:
                total_obtained += cocurricular.total_marks
                if cocurricular.grade_letter == 'F':
                    failed_subjects.append('Co-curricular Activity')

            failed_count = len(failed_subjects)

            if failed_count > 0:
                is_passed = False
                final_gpa = Decimal('0.00')
                overall_grade = 'F'
            else:
                is_passed = True
                total_gp = sum(m.grade_point for m in marks_entries)
                total_subjects_count = marks_entries.count()

                if cocurricular:
                    total_gp += cocurricular.grade_point
                    total_subjects_count += 1

                final_gpa = round(Decimal(total_gp) / Decimal(total_subjects_count), 2) if total_subjects_count > 0 else Decimal('0.00')

                if final_gpa >= Decimal('5.00'):
                    overall_grade = 'A+'
                elif final_gpa >= Decimal('4.00'):
                    overall_grade = 'A'
                elif final_gpa >= Decimal('3.50'):
                    overall_grade = 'A-'
                elif final_gpa >= Decimal('3.00'):
                    overall_grade = 'B'
                elif final_gpa >= Decimal('2.00'):
                    overall_grade = 'C'
                elif final_gpa >= Decimal('1.00'):
                    overall_grade = 'D'
                else:
                    overall_grade = 'F'

            present_days = StudentAttendance.objects.filter(
                student=student,
                date__range=[exam.start_date, exam.end_date],
                status__in=[StudentAttendance.AttendanceStatus.PRESENT, StudentAttendance.AttendanceStatus.LATE]
            ).count()
            absent_days = StudentAttendance.objects.filter(
                student=student,
                date__range=[exam.start_date, exam.end_date],
                status=StudentAttendance.AttendanceStatus.ABSENT
            ).count()

            result_obj, _ = StudentExamResult.objects.update_or_create(
                exam=exam,
                student=student,
                defaults={
                    'section': student.section,
                    'total_full_marks': Decimal('824.00'),
                    'total_obtained_marks': total_obtained,
                    'gpa': final_gpa,
                    'grade_letter': overall_grade,
                    'is_passed': is_passed,
                    'failed_subjects_count': failed_count,
                    'failed_subject_names': ", ".join(failed_subjects),
                    'working_days': working_days,
                    'present_days': present_days,
                    'absent_days': absent_days,
                    'section_position': None,
                    'class_position': None,
                }
            )
            processed_results.append(result_obj)

        passed_in_class = StudentExamResult.objects.filter(
            exam=exam,
            is_passed=True
        ).order_by('-gpa', '-total_obtained_marks', 'student__roll_number')

        for idx, res in enumerate(passed_in_class, start=1):
            res.class_position = idx
            res.save(update_fields=['class_position'])

        sections = AcademicSection.objects.filter(class_level=exam.class_level)
        for sec in sections:
            passed_in_sec = StudentExamResult.objects.filter(
                exam=exam,
                section=sec,
                is_passed=True
            ).order_by('-gpa', '-total_obtained_marks', 'student__roll_number')

            for idx, res in enumerate(passed_in_sec, start=1):
                res.section_position = idx
                res.save(update_fields=['section_position'])

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "message": f"{exam.name} এর ফলাফল ও মেধা তালিকা সফলভাবে প্রসেস করা হয়েছে।",
            "total_processed": len(processed_results),
            "total_passed": passed_in_class.count(),
            "total_failed": len(processed_results) - passed_in_class.count()
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=False, methods=['get'], url_path='my-result')
    def my_result(self, request):
        if request.user.role != User.Role.STUDENT or not hasattr(request.user, 'student_profile'):
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "শুধুমাত্র শিক্ষার্থীরা এই রেজাল্ট দেখতে পারে।"}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        exam_id = request.query_params.get('exam')
        if not exam_id:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "exam প্যারামিটার প্রদান করা আবশ্যক।"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        result = StudentExamResult.objects.filter(
            exam_id=exam_id,
            student=request.user.student_profile,
            exam__is_published=True
        ).first()

        if not result:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "ফলাফল এখনো প্রকাশিত হয়নি অথবা খুঁজে পাওয়া যায়নি।"}, status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND)

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "data": StudentExamResultSerializer(result).data
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class ExaminationReportsViewSet(viewsets.ViewSet):
    """
    পিডিএফ ও প্রিন্ট রিপোর্টের জন্য ডেডিকেটেড অ্যানালিটিক্স ও মার্কশিট ভিউসেট।
    """
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    @action(detail=False, methods=['get'], url_path='progress-report')
    def progress_report(self, request):
        exam_id = request.query_params.get('exam')
        student_id = request.query_params.get('student')
        user = request.user

        if user.role == User.Role.STUDENT and hasattr(user, 'student_profile'):
            student_id = str(user.student_profile.id)

        if not exam_id or not student_id:
            return Response(
                {"success": False, "message": "exam এবং student আইডি প্রদান করা আবশ্যক।"},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST
            )

        try:
            exam = Exam.objects.get(id=exam_id)
            student = StudentProfile.objects.select_related('user', 'section').get(id=student_id)
        except (Exam.DoesNotExist, StudentProfile.DoesNotExist):
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "পরীক্ষা অথবা শিক্ষার্থী খুঁজে পাওয়া যায়নি।"}, status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND)

        overall_result = StudentExamResult.objects.filter(exam=exam, student=student).first()
        subject_entries = SubjectMarksEntry.objects.filter(exam=exam, student=student).select_related('subject')

        subject_rows = []
        for entry in subject_entries:
            highest_mark = SubjectMarksEntry.objects.filter(
                exam=exam,
                subject=entry.subject,
                section__class_level=exam.class_level
            ).aggregate(max_mark=Max('final_total_marks'))['max_mark'] or Decimal('0.00')

            subject_rows.append({
                "subject_name": entry.subject.name,
                "subject_code": entry.subject.code,
                "full_marks": 100.0,
                "cq": float(entry.cq_marks),
                "mcq": float(entry.mcq_marks),
                "practical": float(entry.practical_marks),
                "semester_obtained": float(entry.obtained_semester_marks),
                "semester_converted": float(entry.converted_semester_marks),
                "tutorial": float(entry.tutorial_marks),
                "total_marks": float(entry.final_total_marks),
                "letter_grade": entry.grade_letter,
                "grade_point": float(entry.grade_point),
                "highest_marks": float(highest_mark),
            })

        cocurricular = CoCurricularEntry.objects.filter(exam=exam, student=student).first()
        cocurricular_highest = CoCurricularEntry.objects.filter(
            exam=exam,
            section__class_level=exam.class_level
        ).aggregate(max_cocurr=Max('total_marks'))['max_cocurr'] or Decimal('0.00')

        cocurricular_data = {
            "title": "Co-curricular Activity",
            "full_marks": 124.0,
            "attn": float(cocurricular.attendance_marks) if cocurricular else 0.0,
            "ethics": float(cocurricular.ethics_marks) if cocurricular else 0.0,
            "total_marks": float(cocurricular.total_marks) if cocurricular else 0.0,
            "converted": float(cocurricular.total_marks) if cocurricular else 0.0,
            "letter_grade": cocurricular.grade_letter if cocurricular else 'F',
            "grade_point": float(cocurricular.grade_point) if cocurricular else 0.0,
            "highest_marks": float(cocurricular_highest),
        }

        summary_data = {
            "working_days": overall_result.working_days if overall_result else 0,
            "present_days": overall_result.present_days if overall_result else 0,
            "absent_days": overall_result.absent_days if overall_result else 0,
            "total_marks": float(overall_result.total_full_marks) if overall_result else 824.0,
            "total_obtained": float(overall_result.total_obtained_marks) if overall_result else 0.0,
            "grade": overall_result.grade_letter if overall_result else 'F',
            "gpa": float(overall_result.gpa) if overall_result else 0.00,
            "section_position": overall_result.section_position if overall_result else None,
            "class_position": overall_result.class_position if overall_result else None,
        }

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "institution": {
                "name": "STARLIGHT COLLEGE",
                "address": "Sunargown R/A, Dokshin surma, Sylhet",
                "eiin": "135342",
            },
            "exam_info": {
                "report_title": "Progress Report",
                "exam_name": exam.name,
                "session": exam.session,
            },
            "student_info": {
                "student_name": student.user.get_full_name() or student.user.username,
                "student_id": student.student_unique_id,
                "student_roll": student.roll_number,
                "version": "Bangla",
                "class_level": student.section.get_class_level_display(),
                "section": student.section.section_name,
                "gender": student.get_gender_display(),
                "group": student.section.get_group_display(),
            },
            "grading_scale": [
                {"mark_range": "80-100", "letter_grade": "A+", "grade_point": 5.0},
                {"mark_range": "70-79", "letter_grade": "A", "grade_point": 4.0},
                {"mark_range": "60-69", "letter_grade": "A-", "grade_point": 3.5},
                {"mark_range": "50-59", "letter_grade": "B", "grade_point": 3.0},
                {"mark_range": "40-49", "letter_grade": "C", "grade_point": 2.0},
                {"mark_range": "33-39", "letter_grade": "D", "grade_point": 1.0},
                {"mark_range": "00-32", "letter_grade": "F", "grade_point": 0.0},
            ],
            "subjects": subject_rows,
            "co_curricular": cocurricular_data,
            "overall_summary": summary_data,
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=False, methods=['get'], url_path='subject-analysis')
    def subject_analysis(self, request):
        exam_id = request.query_params.get('exam')
        group = request.query_params.get('group')

        if not exam_id:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "exam আইডি প্রদান করা আবশ্যক।"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        subjects_qs = Subject.objects.filter(routines__section__class_level=Exam.objects.get(id=exam_id).class_level).distinct()
        if group:
            subjects_qs = subjects_qs.filter(group=group.upper())

        analysis_rows = []
        for idx, subj in enumerate(subjects_qs, start=1):
            entries = SubjectMarksEntry.objects.filter(exam_id=exam_id, subject=subj)
            total_students = entries.count()

            if total_students > 0:
                passed_count = entries.filter(is_passed=True).count()
                failed_count = entries.filter(is_passed=False).count()
                pass_pct = round((passed_count / total_students) * 100.0, 2)
                fail_pct = round((failed_count / total_students) * 100.0, 2)
            else:
                passed_count, failed_count, pass_pct, fail_pct = 0, 0, 0.0, 0.0

            analysis_rows.append({
                "sl": idx,
                "subject_name": subj.name,
                "subject_code": subj.code,
                "total_students": total_students,
                "number_of_student_passed": passed_count,
                "pass_percentage": f"{pass_pct}%",
                "number_of_student_failed": failed_count,
                "failed_percentage": f"{fail_pct}%",
            })

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "report_title": "SUBJECT WISE RESULT ANALYSIS",
            "exam_id": exam_id,
            "data": analysis_rows,
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=False, methods=['get'], url_path='failed-statistics')
    def failed_statistics(self, request):
        exam_id = request.query_params.get('exam')
        gender = request.query_params.get('gender', 'MALE').upper()

        if not exam_id:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "exam আইডি প্রদান করা আবশ্যক।"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        failed_results = StudentExamResult.objects.filter(
            exam_id=exam_id,
            is_passed=False,
            student__gender=gender
        ).select_related('student', 'student__user').order_by('student__roll_number')

        failed_rows = []
        for idx, res in enumerate(failed_results, start=1):
            failed_rows.append({
                "sl": idx,
                "student_name": res.student.user.get_full_name() or res.student.user.username,
                "student_id": res.student.student_unique_id,
                "roll": res.student.roll_number,
                "failed_subjects": res.failed_subject_names,
                "total_failed_count": res.failed_subjects_count,
            })

        gender_label = "ছাত্র-সকল গ্রুপ" if gender == 'MALE' else "ছাত্রী-সকল গ্রুপ"

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "report_title": f"বিষয় ভিত্তিক অকৃতকার্য বিবরণী ({gender_label})",
            "gender": gender,
            "total_failed_students": len(failed_rows),
            "data": failed_rows,
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=False, methods=['get'], url_path='merit-list')
    def merit_list(self, request):
        exam_id = request.query_params.get('exam')
        section_id = request.query_params.get('section')

        if not exam_id:
            return Response({"success": False, "message": "exam আইডি প্রদান করা আবশ্যক।"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        passed_results = StudentExamResult.objects.filter(
            exam_id=exam_id,
            is_passed=True
        ).select_related('student', 'student__user', 'section').order_by('class_position')

        if section_id:
            passed_results = passed_results.filter(section_id=section_id).order_by('section_position')

        merit_rows = []
        for idx, res in enumerate(passed_results, start=1):
            merit_rows.append({
                "sl": idx,
                "student_name": res.student.user.get_full_name() or res.student.user.username,
                "student_id": res.student.student_unique_id,
                "roll": res.student.roll_number,
                "section": res.section.section_name,
                "sub_total_full": float(res.total_full_marks),
                "obtained_marks": float(res.total_obtained_marks),
                "grade": res.grade_letter,
                "gpa": float(res.gpa),
                "section_position": res.section_position,
                "class_position": res.class_position,
            })

        return Response({
            "success": True,
            "report_title": "OFFICIAL MERIT LIST",
            "total_passed": len(merit_rows),
            "data": merit_rows,
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

from django.apps import AppConfig

# === FILE: apps\finance\models.py ===
import uuid
from decimal import Decimal
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from django.db import models
from django.utils import timezone
from apps.academics.models import AcademicSection
from apps.common.models import BaseModel
from apps.users_auth.models import StudentProfile, User

class StudentInvoice(BaseModel):
    """
    শিক্ষার্থীর মাসিক বেতন, পরিবহন ফি, অনুপস্থিতির জরিমানা ও বকেয়া ইনভয়েস।
    """
    class InvoiceStatus(models.TextChoices):
        UNPAID = 'UNPAID', 'অপরিশোধিত (Unpaid)'
        PARTIAL = 'PARTIAL', 'আংশিক পরিশোধিত (Partially Paid)'
        PAID = 'PAID', 'সম্পূর্ণ পরিশোধিত (Paid)'

    invoice_number = models.CharField(max_length=50, unique=True, editable=False, db_index=True)
    student = models.ForeignKey(StudentProfile, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='invoices')
    section = models.ForeignKey(AcademicSection, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='invoices')
    
    month = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(help_text="মাসের ক্রমিক নম্বর (১ = জানুয়ারি, ১২ = ডিসেম্বর)")
    year = models.PositiveIntegerField(help_text="যেমন: 2026")
    
    # ফি-এর খাতসমূহ
    tuition_fee = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    transport_fee = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    absence_fine = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00, help_text="প্রতিদিন ১০০ টাকা জরিমানা")
    other_fees = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    
    # মওকুফ বা ছাড় (ইনচার্জের সুপারিশে অধ্যক্ষের অনুমোদনে আসবে)
    waiver_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    
    # আর্থিক মোট এবং পরিশোধ হিসাব
    total_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    paid_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    due_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    
    status = models.CharField(max_length=15, choices=InvoiceStatus.choices, default=InvoiceStatus.UNPAID, db_index=True)
    due_date = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Student Invoice'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Student Invoices'
        unique_together = ('student', 'month', 'year')
        ordering = ['-year', '-month', 'student__roll_number']
        indexes = [
            # বকেয়া ও মাসের ভিত্তিতে চোখের পলকে সার্চ
            models.Index(fields=['student', 'due_amount']),
            models.Index(fields=['year', 'month', 'status']),
        ]

    def calculate_totals(self):
        gross_total = (self.tuition_fee + self.transport_fee + self.absence_fine + self.other_fees)
        self.total_amount = max(Decimal('0.00'), gross_total - self.waiver_amount)
        self.due_amount = max(Decimal('0.00'), self.total_amount - self.paid_amount)

        if self.paid_amount >= self.total_amount and self.total_amount > Decimal('0.00'):
            self.status = self.InvoiceStatus.PAID
        elif self.paid_amount > Decimal('0.00'):
            self.status = self.InvoiceStatus.PARTIAL
        else:
            self.status = self.InvoiceStatus.UNPAID

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self.invoice_number:
            unique_suffix = uuid.uuid4().hex[:6].upper()
            self.invoice_number = f"INV-{self.year}{self.month:02d}-{unique_suffix}"
        self.calculate_totals()
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.invoice_number} | {self.student.student_unique_id} - {self.month}/{self.year} ({self.status})"


class PaymentTransaction(BaseModel):
    """
    ফি জমার ট্রানজেকশন ও মানি রিসিট (ক্যাশ, বিকাশ, ব্যাংক ইত্যাদি)।
    """
    class PaymentMethod(models.TextChoices):
        CASH = 'CASH', 'ক্যাশ কাউন্টার (Cash)'
        BKASH = 'BKASH', 'বিকাশ (bKash)'
        NAGAD = 'NAGAD', 'নগদ (Nagad)'
        BANK = 'BANK', 'ব্যাংক ট্রান্সফার (Bank)'
        OTHER = 'OTHER', 'অন্যান্য (Other)'

    receipt_no = models.CharField(max_length=50, unique=True, editable=False, db_index=True)
    invoice = models.ForeignKey(StudentInvoice, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='payments')
    amount_paid = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2)
    payment_method = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=PaymentMethod.choices, default=PaymentMethod.CASH)
    transaction_reference = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, help_text="bKash TrxID বা ব্যাংক স্লিপ নম্বর")
    collected_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)
    remarks = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Payment Transaction'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Payment Transactions'
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self.receipt_no:
            self.receipt_no = f"REC-{timezone.now().strftime('%Y%m%d')}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6].upper()}"
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.receipt_no} - {self.amount_paid} BDT ({self.payment_method})"


class FineWaiverApplication(BaseModel):
    """
    অনুপস্থিতির জরিমানা বা ফি মওকুফের ২-ধাপের আবেদন:
    Student আবেদন ➔ In-Charge শিক্ষক সুপারিশ ➔ Principal চূড়ান্ত অনুমোদন (স্বয়ংক্রিয়ভাবে বিলে ডিসকাউন্ট যোগ হবে)।
    """
    class InChargeWaiverStatus(models.TextChoices):
        PENDING = 'PENDING', 'পর্যালোচনায় আছে (Pending)'
        RECOMMENDED = 'RECOMMENDED', 'সুপারিশকৃত (Recommended)'
        REJECTED = 'REJECTED', 'সুপারিশ বাতিল (Rejected)'

    class PrincipalWaiverStatus(models.TextChoices):
        PENDING = 'PENDING', 'অপেক্ষমাণ (Pending)'
        APPROVED = 'APPROVED', 'মওকুফ অনুমোদিত (Approved)'
        REJECTED = 'REJECTED', 'মওকুফ বাতিল (Rejected)'

    invoice = models.ForeignKey(StudentInvoice, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='waiver_applications')
    student = models.ForeignKey(StudentProfile, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='waiver_applications')
    reason = models.TextField(help_text="অসুস্থতা বা পারিবারিক কারণে জরিমানা মওকুফের কারণ")
    
    # ধাপ ১: ইনচার্জ সুপারিশ
    incharge_status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=InChargeWaiverStatus.choices, default=InChargeWaiverStatus.PENDING, db_index=True)
    recommended_waiver_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    incharge_remarks = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)
    reviewed_by_incharge = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='incharge_waivers')
    incharge_reviewed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    # ধাপ ২: প্রিন্সিপালের চূড়ান্ত সিদ্ধান্ত
    principal_status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=PrincipalWaiverStatus.choices, default=PrincipalWaiverStatus.PENDING, db_index=True)
    approved_waiver_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    principal_remarks = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)
    approved_by_principal = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='principal_waivers')
    principal_reviewed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Fine Waiver Application'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Fine Waiver Applications'
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.student.student_unique_id} | Inv: {self.invoice.invoice_number} | Status: {self.principal_status}"
    

class ExpenseCategory(BaseModel):
    """
    কলেজের খরচের খাত (যেমন: বিদ্যুৎ ও ইউটিলিটি, শিক্ষক আপ্যায়ন, ল্যাব সরঞ্জাম, মেরামত ও রক্ষণাবেক্ষণ ইত্যাদি)।
    """
    name = models.CharField(max_length=100, unique=True)
    description = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Expense Category'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Expense Categories'
        ordering = ['name']

    def __str__(self):
        return self.name


class CollegeExpense(BaseModel):
    """
    কলেজের দৈনন্দিন খরচের ভাউচার মডেল।
    অ্যাকাউন্ট্যান্ট ড্রাফট এন্ট্রি দেবেন এবং প্রিন্সিপাল অনুমোদন করবেন।
    """
    class ExpenseStatus(models.TextChoices):
        PENDING = 'PENDING', 'অপেক্ষমাণ (Pending Approval)'
        APPROVED = 'APPROVED', 'অনুমোদিত (Approved)'
        REJECTED = 'REJECTED', 'বাতিল (Rejected)'

    category = models.ForeignKey(
        ExpenseCategory,
        on_delete=models.PROTECT,
        related_name='expenses'
    )
    title = models.CharField(max_length=200, help_text="খরচের সংক্ষিপ্ত শিরোনাম")
    amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, help_text="টাকার পরিমাণ")
    voucher_no = models.CharField(max_length=50, unique=True, editable=False, db_index=True)
    voucher_image = models.ImageField(upload_to='vouchers/%Y/%m/', null=True, blank=True)
    
    expense_date = models.DateField(default=timezone.localdate, db_index=True)
    description = models.TextField(blank=True, help_text="খরচের বিস্তারিত ব্যাখ্যা বা ভেন্ডরের তথ্য")
    
    status = models.CharField(
        max_length=15,
        choices=ExpenseStatus.choices,
        default=ExpenseStatus.PENDING,
        db_index=True
    )
    
    recorded_by = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name='recorded_expenses'
    )
    approved_by = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name='approved_expenses'
    )
    approved_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    rejection_reason = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'College Expense'
        verbose_name_plural = 'College Expenses'
        ordering = ['-expense_date', '-created_at']

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self.voucher_no:
            self.voucher_no = f"VCH-{timezone.now().strftime('%Y%m%d')}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6].upper()}"
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"[{self.voucher_no}] {self.title} - {self.amount} BDT ({self.status})"


class TeacherPayroll(BaseModel):
    """
    শিক্ষকদের মাসিক পে-রোল শিট মডেল (অ্যাকাউন্ট্যান্ট জেনারেট করবেন -> প্রিন্সিপাল অ্যাপ্রুভ করবেন -> ডিজবার্স হবে)।
    """
    class PayrollStatus(models.TextChoices):
        PENDING = 'PENDING', 'অপেক্ষমাণ (Pending Approval)'
        APPROVED = 'APPROVED', 'প্রিন্সিপাল কর্তৃক অনুমোদিত (Approved)'
        DISBURSED = 'DISBURSED', 'পরিশোধিত (Disbursed/Paid)'
        REJECTED = 'REJECTED', 'বাতিল (Rejected)'

    teacher = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='payrolls',
        limit_choices_to={'role': User.Role.TEACHER}
    )
    month = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(help_text="মাসের ক্রমিক নম্বর (১-১২)")
    year = models.PositiveIntegerField()
    
    base_salary = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2)
    bonus_allowance = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    deductions = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    net_salary = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=PayrollStatus.choices, default=PayrollStatus.PENDING, db_index=True)
    payment_method = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=PaymentTransaction.PaymentMethod.choices, default=PaymentTransaction.PaymentMethod.BANK)
    payment_reference = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True, help_text="চেক নম্বর বা ব্যাংক ট্রানজেকশন আইডি")
    
    approved_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='approved_payrolls')
    approved_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    disbursed_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='disbursed_payrolls')
    disbursed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    remarks = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Teacher Payroll'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Teacher Payrolls'
        unique_together = ('teacher', 'month', 'year')
        ordering = ['-year', '-month']

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        self.net_salary = max(Decimal('0.00'), (self.base_salary + self.bonus_allowance) - self.deductions)
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.teacher.get_full_name()} - {self.month}/{self.year} (Net: {self.net_salary} BDT)"


class TeacherSalaryClaim(BaseModel):
    """
    শিক্ষক কর্তৃক অগ্রিম বা বিশেষ বেতন ক্লেইমের মডেল (শিক্ষক আবেদন ➔ প্রিন্সিপাল অ্যাপ্রুভ ➔ অ্যাকাউন্ট্যান্ট ডিজবার্স)।
    """
    class ClaimStatus(models.TextChoices):
        PENDING = 'PENDING', 'পর্যালোচনায় আছে (Pending)'
        APPROVED_BY_PRINCIPAL = 'APPROVED', 'অধ্যক্ষ কর্তৃক অনুমোদিত (Approved by Principal)'
        DISBURSED = 'DISBURSED', 'অ্যাকাউন্ট কর্তৃক পরিশোধিত (Disbursed)'
        REJECTED = 'REJECTED', 'বাতিল (Rejected)'

    class ClaimType(models.TextChoices):
        REGULAR = 'REGULAR', 'মাসিক নিয়মিত বেতন (Regular Monthly Salary)'
        ADVANCE = 'ADVANCE', 'অগ্রিম বেতন দাবি (Salary Advance)'
        SPECIAL = 'SPECIAL', 'বিশেষ সম্মানী বা বোনাস (Special Allowance)'

    teacher = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='salary_claims',
        limit_choices_to={'role': User.Role.TEACHER}
    )
    month = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField()
    year = models.PositiveIntegerField()
    claim_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=ClaimType.choices, default=ClaimType.REGULAR)
    requested_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2)
    reason = models.TextField(help_text="দাবি বা অগ্রিমের কারণ")

    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=ClaimStatus.choices, default=ClaimStatus.PENDING, db_index=True)
    principal_remarks = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)
    approved_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='approved_salary_claims')
    approved_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    
    disbursed_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True, related_name='disbursed_salary_claims')
    disbursed_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    payment_reference = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Teacher Salary Claim'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Teacher Salary Claims'
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.teacher.get_full_name()} | {self.claim_type} ({self.requested_amount} BDT) - {self.status}"


class TransportVehicle(BaseModel):
    """
    কলেজের পরিবহন/গাড়ির তালিকা ও দৈনিক রুট ভিত্তিক চুক্তি।
    """
    vehicle_number = models.CharField(max_length=50, unique=True, help_text="যেমন: ঢাকা মেট্রো-চ-১১-২২৩৩")
    driver_name = models.CharField(max_length=150)
    driver_phone = models.CharField(max_length=20, db_index=True)
    route_name = models.CharField(max_length=200, help_text="যেমন: রুট ১: আম্বরখানা ➔ কলেজ ক্যাম্পাস")
    stoppages = models.TextField(blank=True, help_text="গাড়ির স্টপেজসমূহ, যেমন: আম্বরখানা ➔ চৌহাট্টা ➔ বন্দর ➔ ক্যাম্পাস")
    daily_rate = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, help_text="প্রতিদিন গাড়ি চললে নির্ধারিত বিল (BDT)")
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Transport Vehicle'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Transport Vehicles'
        ordering = ['route_name']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.vehicle_number} ({self.route_name}) - {self.daily_rate} Tk/day"


class VehicleDailyLog(BaseModel):
    """
    গাড়ির দৈনিক ট্রিপ ও উপস্থিতি লগ (গাড়ি না চললে ওই দিনের বিল নেই)।
    """
    vehicle = models.ForeignKey(TransportVehicle, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='daily_logs')
    date = models.DateField(default=timezone.localdate, db_index=True)
    ran_today = models.BooleanField(default=True, help_text="আজ গাড়ি চলাচল করেছে কি না")
    trips_count = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=2, help_text="সাধারণত আসা ও যাওয়া মিলিয়ে ২টি ট্রিপ")
    remarks = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, help_text="গাড়ি নষ্ট থাকলে বা ট্রিপ মিসের কারণ")
    recorded_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Vehicle Daily Log'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Vehicle Daily Logs'
        unique_together = ('vehicle', 'date')
        ordering = ['-date']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.vehicle.vehicle_number} - {self.date} (Ran: {self.ran_today})"


class DriverAdvanceDeduction(BaseModel):
    """
    ড্রাইভার কর্তৃক গ্যাস/তেল বাবদ বা ব্যক্তিগত কারণে কলেজ থেকে অগ্রিম টাকা গ্রহণ (যা মাসের বিল থেকে কাটা যাবে)।
    """
    vehicle = models.ForeignKey(TransportVehicle, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='advances')
    date = models.DateField(default=timezone.localdate, db_index=True)
    amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, help_text="গৃহীত অগ্রিম টাকার পরিমাণ")
    purpose = models.CharField(max_length=255, help_text="যেমন: সিএনজি গ্যাস রিফিল বা টায়ার মেরামত")
    voucher_no = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True)
    recorded_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Driver Advance / Deduction'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Driver Advances / Deductions'
        ordering = ['-date']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.vehicle.vehicle_number} | {self.amount} BDT ({self.purpose})"


class VehicleMonthlyBill(BaseModel):
    """
    গাড়ির মালিক বা ড্রাইভারের মাসিক নিট বিল মডেল:
    (যতদিন গাড়ি চলেছে * দৈনিক রেট) - তেলের অগ্রিম টাকা = নিট প্রদেয় বিল
    """
    class BillStatus(models.TextChoices):
        DRAFT = 'DRAFT', 'খসড়া (Draft)'
        APPROVED = 'APPROVED', 'অনুমোদিত (Approved)'
        SETTLED = 'SETTLED', 'পরিশোধিত (Settled/Paid)'

    bill_number = models.CharField(max_length=50, unique=True, editable=False)
    vehicle = models.ForeignKey(TransportVehicle, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='monthly_bills')
    month = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField()
    year = models.PositiveIntegerField()

    total_days_run = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)
    daily_rate = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2)
    gross_bill = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    total_deductions = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    net_payable = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)

    status = models.CharField(max_length=15, choices=BillStatus.choices, default=BillStatus.DRAFT, db_index=True)
    settled_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)
    settled_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    payment_reference = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Vehicle Monthly Bill'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Vehicle Monthly Bills'
        unique_together = ('vehicle', 'month', 'year')
        ordering = ['-year', '-month']

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self.bill_number:
            self.bill_number = f"VBILL-{self.year}{self.month:02d}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6].upper()}"
        self.gross_bill = self.total_days_run * self.daily_rate
        self.net_payable = max(Decimal('0.00'), self.gross_bill - self.total_deductions)
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.bill_number} | {self.vehicle.vehicle_number} ({self.net_payable} BDT) - {self.status}"


class VehicleBreakdownAlert(BaseModel):
    """
    মাঝপথে গাড়ি নষ্ট হওয়ার ইমার্জেন্সি রিপোর্ট।
    ড্রাইভারের কলের পর অ্যাকাউন্ট্যান্ট বা প্রিন্সিপাল অফিসিয়ালি নোটিশ জারি করবেন।
    """
    vehicle = models.ForeignKey(TransportVehicle, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='breakdowns')
    location = models.CharField(max_length=200, help_text="যে স্থানে গাড়ি নষ্ট হয়েছে")
    reason = models.TextField(help_text="যান্ত্রিক ত্রুটির কারণ")
    alert_message = models.TextField(help_text="শিক্ষার্থী ও অভিভাবকদের পাঠানো বার্তা")
    is_resolved = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    resolved_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    reported_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Vehicle Breakdown Alert'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Vehicle Breakdown Alerts'
        ordering = ['-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"[{self.vehicle.vehicle_number}] {self.location} - Resolved: {self.is_resolved}"


class InchargeCashHandover(BaseModel):
    """
    শ্রেণি ইনচার্জ কর্তৃক সারাদিনে শিক্ষার্থীদের থেকে আদায়কৃত ক্যাশ টাকা অ্যাকাউন্ট্যান্টের কাছে বুঝিয়ে দেওয়ার রিকনসিলিয়েশন মডেল।
    """
    class HandoverStatus(models.TextChoices):
        PENDING = 'PENDING', 'অপেক্ষমাণ (Pending Verification)'
        VERIFIED = 'VERIFIED', 'গৃহীত ও যাচাইকৃত (Verified & Received)'
        DISCREPANCY = 'DISCREPANCY', 'টাকার হিসাবে অমিল (Discrepancy Found)'

    in_charge = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name='cash_handovers',
        limit_choices_to={'role': User.Role.TEACHER}
    )
    handover_date = models.DateField(default=timezone.localdate, db_index=True)
    amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, help_text="ইনচার্জ কর্তৃক বুঝিয়ে দেওয়া নগদ টাকা")
    status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=HandoverStatus.choices, default=HandoverStatus.PENDING, db_index=True)
    
    received_by = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name='received_handovers',
        help_text="যে অ্যাকাউন্ট্যান্ট ক্যাশ বুঝে নিলেন"
    )
    received_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    notes = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, help_text="হিসাব সংক্রান্ত কোনো মন্তব্য")

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'In-Charge Cash Handover'
        verbose_name_plural = 'In-Charge Cash Handovers'
        ordering = ['-handover_date', '-created_at']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.in_charge.get_full_name()} -> {self.amount} BDT [{self.handover_date}] ({self.status})"


class BankDeposit(BaseModel):
    """
    কলেজের ড্রয়ার থেকে ব্যাংকে টাকা জমা দেওয়ার রেকর্ড ও ব্যাংক ডিপোজিট স্লিপ মডেল (Cash to Bank Transfer)।
    """
    bank_name = models.CharField(max_length=150, help_text="যেমন: সোনালী ব্যাংক, ডাচ-বাংলা ব্যাংক")
    account_number = models.CharField(max_length=50, help_text="কলেজের ব্যাংক অ্যাকাউন্ট নম্বর")
    deposit_date = models.DateField(default=timezone.localdate, db_index=True)
    amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, help_text="ব্যাংকে জমাকৃত টাকার পরিমাণ")
    
    slip_number = models.CharField(max_length=100, help_text="ব্যাংক জমার স্লিপ বা ট্রানজেকশন নম্বর")
    slip_image = models.ImageField(upload_to='bank_slips/%Y/%m/', null=True, blank=True, help_text="ব্যাংক রসিদের ছবি")
    
    deposited_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)
    notes = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Bank Deposit'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Bank Deposits'
        ordering = ['-deposit_date']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.bank_name} ({self.account_number}) - {self.amount} BDT [{self.deposit_date}]"


class Supplier(BaseModel):
    """
    কলেজের সরবরাহকারী বা দোকানদারদের তথ্য (যেমন: প্রিন্টিং প্রেস, ফার্নিচার দোকান, ল্যাব ভেন্ডর)।
    """
    name = models.CharField(max_length=150, unique=True, help_text="দোকান বা ভেন্ডরের নাম")
    service_type = models.CharField(max_length=100, help_text="যেমন: প্রিন্টিং প্রেস, ফার্নিচার, স্টেশনারি")
    contact_person = models.CharField(max_length=150, blank=True)
    phone = models.CharField(max_length=20, db_index=True)
    address = models.TextField(blank=True)
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Supplier / Vendor'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Suppliers & Vendors'
        ordering = ['name']

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.name} ({self.service_type})"


class SupplierPurchase(BaseModel):
    """
    ভেন্ডর থেকে কেনাকাটা বা কাজের বিল।
    """
    supplier = models.ForeignKey(Supplier, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='purchases')
    invoice_no = models.CharField(max_length=100, unique=True)
    purchase_date = models.DateField(default=timezone.localdate)
    item_description = models.TextField(help_text="যেমন: ৫০টি পরীক্ষার ব্যানার ও প্রশ্নপত্র প্রিন্টিং")
    total_bill = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2)
    paid_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)
    due_amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2, default=0.00)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Supplier Purchase'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Supplier Purchases'
        ordering = ['-purchase_date']

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        self.due_amount = max(Decimal('0.00'), self.total_bill - self.paid_amount)
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.supplier.name} - Inv: {self.invoice_no} (Due: {self.due_amount})"


class SupplierPayment(BaseModel):
    """
    দোকানদার/ভেন্ডরদের বকেয়া কিস্তির পরিশোধ মডেল।
    """
    purchase = models.ForeignKey(SupplierPurchase, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='payments')
    payment_date = models.DateField(default=timezone.localdate)
    amount_paid = models.DecimalField(max_digits=12, decimal_places=2)
    payment_method = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=PaymentTransaction.PaymentMethod.choices, default=PaymentTransaction.PaymentMethod.CASH)
    payment_receipt_no = models.CharField(max_length=100, blank=True)
    paid_by = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)
    remarks = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'Supplier Payment'
        verbose_name_plural = 'Supplier Payments'
        ordering = ['-payment_date']

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)
        # মূল পারচেজ বিলে পরিশোধ সমন্বয় করা
        p = self.purchase
        total_paid = p.payments.aggregate(total=models.Sum('amount_paid'))['total'] or Decimal('0.00')
        p.paid_amount = total_paid
        p.save()

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Payment of {self.amount_paid} to {self.purchase.supplier.name}"

            raise serializers.ValidationError("জমার পরিমাণ ০ টাকার বেশি হতে হবে।")
        return value
